- `P1_identity_and_lock`: canonical destination identity and lock scope match approval.
- `P2_revalidation`: source, staging, destination, and preserved entry types/bytes are checked under lock immediately before swap.
- `P3_monotonic_commit`: durable commit can only be forward-repaired, never rolled back.
- `P4_fsync_and_receipts`: required file/directory durability steps and terminal receipt verify.
- `P5_authority_and_diversity`: destructive plan has human approval; critical recovery has independent Terra audit; Sol forbidden.
- Success means transaction reconciliation, not mathematical or campaign success.

**Default tiers**

- Luna executes deterministic hashing, identity, fsync, and reducer-directed steps.
- Terra maintains the transaction implementation and audits critical recovery.
- Human authorizes destructive or changed plans.
- Sol disabled.

**Escalation**

- Malformed receipt/path/identity mismatch → fail closed with no mutation.
- Implementation defect → Terra maintenance and fault-injection replay.
- Authenticity, destructive-policy, or irreconcilable committed-state dispute → human adjudication.
- Never escalate publication recovery to Sol.

### Anti-patterns

- Generic prompt labeled as a playbook without typed inputs, step DAG, validators, receipts, gates, invalidation, and terminal derivation.
- A model choosing its own tool, model tier, timeout, retries, path, parallelism, security capability, failure policy, continuation, or solved status.
- Calling Sol because work is expensive, long, cross-module, or failed operationally; Sol is only evidence-admitted frontier planning/revision or separately admitted critical mathematical adjudication.
- Treating exit 0, `PASS`, controller `complete`, campaign completion, publication, model confidence, compiled source, or audit-process success as mathematical closure.
- Validating one read and hashing another; pathname validate-then-use; parsing receipts by filename/basename/field presence; arbitrary strings as evidence.
- Retrying blank, malformed, timed-out, sandbox-failed, or missing-dependency output with a stronger model rather than deterministic recovery.
- Whole-campaign rerun for one failed obligation; discarding valid independent evidence; resetting attempts or collapsing retry invocations.
- Self-review, shared hidden context, producer-as-adjudicator, writable canonical workspace for verifier, or self-asserted `independent`/`cold` labels.
- Silent statement repair: dropping assumptions, changing quantifiers/domains/branches/endpoint strictness, replacing source perimeter with modeled perimeter, or substituting a nearby declaration.
- Promoting numeric telemetry, MPFR smoke output, source prose, or model review to exact/formal evidence; averaging conflicting evidence classes.
- Reusing stale output/handoff because its path exists; accepting output not bound to current nonce/input/contract/toolchain/policy; keeping old approval after dependency change.
- Deleting or overwriting failed, stale, rejected, or corrupt records instead of append-only tombstones and lineage.
- Full-state journal replay and whole-tree hashing on every step when incremental event/evidence commits suffice; reporting configured ceilings as consumed compute.
- Fail-open continuation from optional gate failure, `failure_policy=continue`, missing decision, process success, or planner prose.
- Using human review for routine transitions or using human approval to waive formal rejection, missing evidence, unauthorized capabilities, or deterministic counterexamples.
- Mutating a v1 run in place or emitting new v1 schemas; silently restoring defaults/security fields during migration.
- Calling a partial certificate global, calling a sufficient decimal cutoff sharp, calling a modeled theorem source-level, or identifying topological frontier with reduced boundary by terminology alone.
- Publishing or replaying against a mutable canonical workspace; allowing recovery/planning to mutate before full preflight and lock acquisition.
- Opaque review summaries without complete findings, identities, attack trace, evidence refs, and closure verdict.
- A playbook result with `succeeded` but missing expected-record manifest, launch/terminal receipts, output-schema validation, freshness check, diversity computation, or limitations.

### Bootstrap and release order

Later playbook registration or enablement is conditional on completion of every preceding foundation. The normative order is:

1. 1. Freeze and approve the v2 status vocabulary, failure codes, evidence classes, model-role policy, human authority boundaries, and local POSIX/Linux support/threat model.
2. 2. Implement shared strict schema/value primitives, canonical encoding, `SchemaRegistry`, `ArtifactRefV2`, raw-byte parse/hash, and read-only `v1_import`; prohibit all native v1 emission.
3. 3. Implement authoritative append-only event store, CAS reducer, state projection, trusted head/checkpoint, receipt lifecycle, and offline full-chain audit.
4. 4. Implement immutable/content-addressed artifact store, safe descriptor-relative filesystem capabilities, exact-set evidence commits, invalidation DAG/tombstones, and stable snapshot verification.
5. 5. Implement unified execution supervisor/resource ledger with launch-before-side-effect receipts, sandbox attestations, process identity/reaping, bounded streams, checkpoints, watchdogs, and no daemon work after return.
6. 6. Implement playbook compiler/controller: registry match, preconditions, deterministic routing, budget governor, serial scheduler, universal gates U0–U6, output validators, diversity computation, and terminal derivation.
7. 7. Register and test `ops.no_output_process_recovery` and `ops.authoritative_state_and_stale_artifact_recovery` first; replay the 3,601.921 s, 1,500.962 s, 3,007.963 s, and 643 s incidents with deterministic fault scenarios.
8. 8. Register and fault-test `ops.publication_transaction_recovery`, including every prepared/swap/fsync/commit/rollback cut and malformed-receipt zero-mutation behavior.
9. 9. Register `math.receipt_first_formal_audit`; close Lean trusted-channel/truncation/toolchain issues and establish clean target-including build/signature/axiom receipts.
10. 10. Register `math.typed_claim_dag_reconciliation`; import audited v1 mathematical records, enforce permanent corrections, and generate the authoritative scoped claim inventory.
11. 11. Register `math.proof_producing_certificate_generation`, then `math.equal_area_fold_root_topology` and `math.full_domain_gamma_certificate`; run bounded pilot certificates before global claims.
12. 12. Register `math.frontier_to_reduced_boundary` only after source perimeter semantics are human-frozen and formal baseline receipts exist; enable Sol planning only through the frontier eligibility gate.
13. 13. Add L0–L7 test layers: schema/property, model-based crash matrix, subprocess, adversarial filesystem, privileged Linux containment, real Lean/toolchain oracle, end-to-end security/autonomy, and mutation/fuzz. Every test carries invariant/incident metadata.
14. 14. Qualify release on one immutable commit: zero unreceipted invocations, zero null-plan execution, zero fail-open continuation, zero stale-output acceptance, zero false closure, complete capability-aware release matrix, and no skipped in-scope P0 gate.

## 12. V2 minimal architecture

V2 is a **clean native rebuild deployed beside v1 as a strangler replacement**. It is neither an in-place refactor of the v1 monoliths nor a repository-wide big bang. The native core contains exactly these modules:

| Module | Sole responsibilities | Required public interfaces | Dependency rule |
|---|---|---|---|
| `v2.contracts` | Schema registry; canonical encoding; bounded immutable values; identifiers, hashes, sizes, paths, DAGs, status enums; source/effective/run configuration snapshots; feature and handoff registrations; migration metadata. No I/O or execution. | `SchemaRegistry`; `Identifier`; `SafeRunId`; `WorkspacePath`; `RetainedPath`; `Sha256`; `ArtifactType`; `DirectedAcyclicGraph`; `CampaignSourceManifestV2.load_bytes`; `EffectiveCampaignConfigV2.compile`; `ResolvedProjectSnapshotV2`; `RunSnapshotV2`; `FeatureRegistration`; `HandoffRegistration` | Imports no v1 code and performs no filesystem mutation. All other native modules depend on it. |
| `v2.compat_v1` | Quarantined exact-behavior v1 readers and one-way import. Its public compatibility namespace is `v1_import`. It preserves observed v1 accept/reject behavior, defects, DTO values, ordering, errors, statuses, and exit mappings, while recording migration decisions. | Existing symbol-shaped readers, including `CampaignSpec.load`, `CampaignSpec.load_resolved`, `ProjectSpec.load`, model `.parse` methods, handoff/claim/semantic loaders, evidence/journal/registry/publication readers; `import_bundle_v1(snapshot) -> MigrationResultV2` | **Only** native v2 package allowed to import v1 schema implementations. Cannot serialize v1, invoke v1 mutation paths, or accept shallow mixed-version objects. |
| `v2.store` | Safe filesystem; immutable content-addressed artifacts and snapshot roots; append-only typed events; validated commit heads; pure state projection boundary; workspace transactions; evidence commits; offline v1 chain audit. | `SafeFilesystem`; `ArtifactRefV2`; `ArtifactStore`; `WorkspaceTransaction`; `CampaignStateStore.append_transition(expected_sequence, transition)`; `CampaignStateStore.snapshot(shared=True)`; `StateProjectionV2`; `EvidenceCommitV2`; `verify_evidence_snapshot`; `audit_full_chain` | Depends only on `v2.contracts`. Descriptor-relative/no-follow access is mandatory; path strings never confer authority. |
| `v2.control` | Pure state machine; serial scheduler; budget governor; retry/invalidation closure; approval transitions; campaign application service; status and exit-code derivation. Queries never mutate. | `RunEventV2`; `RunStateV2`; `StateMachine.reduce`; `Scheduler.ready_set`; `Scheduler.select`; `BudgetGovernor.preflight`; `BudgetGovernor.authorize_attempt`; `CampaignApplicationService`; `HandoffApprovalState` | Depends on contracts and store ports. Concrete execution, assurance, and publication implementations are injected; no circular imports. No model may set policy, continuation legality, closure, or solved status. |
| `v2.execution` | One supervisor, one sandbox abstraction, and one transactional resource ledger for command, agent, Lean, and Herdr adapters. | `ExecutionSupervisor.run(ExecutionRequestV2) -> ExecutionResultV2`; `SandboxBackend`; `SandboxAttestationV2`; `ResourceLedger.intent/attach/transition/reconcile/cas_delete`; `ShutdownCoordinator.stop_run`; `ShutdownCoordinator.stop_all`; `ComputeReceiptV2` | Depends on contracts and store. Every request uses capability handles and an attempt nonce. Every owned process identity binds PID, start time, process-group ID, session ID, boot/generation identity, and available pidfd/capability evidence. No pump thread or owned child may outlive return. `stop_run` and `stop_all` are idempotent, resume from durable phase receipts, and use one global deadline covering freeze, process/unit termination, Herdr close, reconciliation, state commit, and evidence commit; repeated invocation converges without duplicate effects. |
| `v2.assurance` | Registered one-read handoffs; claims; Lean receipts; semantic review; obligations; provenance and approval joins; inspection; compute derivation; final outcome derivation. | `ValidatedHandoffV2`; `ClaimProposalBundleV2`; `VerifierVerdictBundleV2`; `ClaimLedgerV3`; `LeanVerificationReceiptV2`; `SemanticReviewV2`; `ObligationEvidenceV2`; `CampaignOutcomeV3.derive`; `inspect_snapshot(...) -> AssuranceReportV3`; `ComputeLedgerV2.derive` | Depends on contracts and verified store snapshots. Callers cannot provide `solved`; failed producers receive no closure credit. |
| `v2.publication` | Destructive publication and project-repository transactions: canonical planning, approval binding, destination locking, private destination-dirfd-anchored same-parent/same-filesystem staging, swap/backup/fsync, reduction, and idempotent recovery. | `PublicationPlanV2`; `PublicationApprovalV2`; `PublicationTransactionV2.execute`; `PublicationTransactionV2.recover`; `ProjectRepository.commit` | Consumes only verified immutable refs and evidence commits, never live workspace paths. Terminal outcomes are exactly `committed`, `committed_recovery_required`, `rolled_back`, or `failed`. |
| `v2.facade` | Thin Python API, CLI adapters, compatibility names, explicit schema negotiation, result rendering, and top-level composition. | Existing required Python exports and command names; `CommandResultEnvelope`; explicit `--format/--schema` selection; native campaign composition | May compose all native modules. It contains no durable state logic. Regime, autonomy, and benchmark support may enter later only as adapters over committed v2 APIs. |

### Core record and authority rules

- Every durable v2 object has a unique `artifact_type`, explicit schema version, strictness/extension policy, canonical encoding, producer and minimum-reader identity, identity bindings, stable error code/path, and a declared migration path. No native v2 object is unversioned or uses a v1 schema identity.
- `ArtifactRefV2` is the sole durable artifact reference: namespace, digest/Merkle root, size, kind/media type, producer run/stage/attempt, schema/type, locator/display path, and assurance class. Mutable paths are never artifact identity.
- Initial trust scope is a declared local trusted-writer boundary. Hashes, producer identities, approvals, and digest bindings establish consistency and replay binding there; they do **not** establish cross-principal authenticity against a writer able to replace the bundle. `AssuranceReportV3` must expose separate integrity and authenticity labels and mark cross-owner/export authenticity unsupported until an approved signature/MAC or equivalent ownership mechanism exists.
- `RunEventV2` is the sole state authority: record type/version, campaign/run IDs, monotonic sequence, commit ID, predecessor/checksum, transition, producer implementation digest, idempotency key, actor, and recorded time. `state.json` is only a digest-bound `StateProjectionV2` and is always rebuildable from valid events.
- `EvidenceCommitV2` binds campaign/run identity, state sequence/digest, event head, configuration root, input root, resource-intent head, artifact root, exact retained set, and producer/toolchain identities.
- Parse and hash always consume the same bounded immutable bytes. Certified reads use capabilities or verified immutable refs, not resolve/check/use pathnames.
- The scheduler is serial. Ready-set computation, selection, dependency closure, invalidation, and budget decisions are pure and recorded.
- Execution uses one lifecycle: capability validation → durable launch intent → sandbox preparation → spawn → process identity attachment → bounded byte-stream drain/hash → deadline/quota decision → termination/reaping → cleanup reconciliation → atomic attempt publication.

### Orthogonal status vocabulary

No generic `complete`, `success`, or `PASS` may cross dimensions. Public state exposes independent fields:

| Dimension | Vocabulary and meaning |
|---|---|
| `implementation_status` | `NO_IMPLEMENTATION_EVIDENCE`, `EDIT_LANDED`, `SOURCE_VERIFIED`, `CODE_COMPLETE`. This field describes implementation completeness only. |
| `focused_test_status` | `FOCUSED_TEST_NOT_RUN`, `FOCUSED_TEST_FAILED`, `FOCUSED_TEST_PASSED`; backed by one or more immutable receipts. |
| `full_gate_status` | `FULL_GATES_NOT_RUN`, `FULL_GATES_FAILED`, `FULL_GATES_PASSED`; scoped to one frozen commit and declared gate set. No value implies either of the other two fields. |
| `controller_status` | `CONTROLLER_READY`, `CONTROLLER_RUNNING`, `CONTROLLER_STOPPED`, `CONTROLLER_FAILED`, `CONTROLLER_INTERRUPTED`, `CONTROLLER_TERMINAL`. Terminality does not imply campaign completion. |
| `attempt_lifecycle_status` | `PENDING`, `RUNNABLE`, `STARTING`, `RUNNING`, `TERMINAL`, `SKIPPED`, `CANCELLED`. |
| `process_status` | `PROCESS_NOT_RUN`, `PROCESS_RUNNING`, `PROCESS_SUCCEEDED`, `PROCESS_FAILED`, `TIMED_OUT`, `INTERRUPTED`, `CANCELLED`. |
| `artifact_validation_status` | `ARTIFACT_VALIDATION_NOT_RUN`, `ARTIFACT_VALIDATION_FAILED`, `ARTIFACT_VALIDATED`. |
| `semantic_status` | `SEMANTIC_NOT_REVIEWED`, `SEMANTIC_ACCEPTED`, `SEMANTIC_REJECTED`, `SEMANTIC_BLOCKED`. `semantic_status` is independent of `process_status`, so `PROCESS_SUCCEEDED` and `SEMANTIC_REJECTED` may be retained simultaneously. |
| `campaign_status` | `CAMPAIGN_ACTIVE`, `CAMPAIGN_BLOCKED`, `CAMPAIGN_TERMINAL`, `CAMPAIGN_COMPLETE`. Completion requires an adopted plan, acceptable required-stage outcomes, continued mandatory gates, finalized evidence, and reconciled children. |
| `mathematical_status` | `MATHEMATICAL_UNSOLVED`, `CONTRACT_UNDISCHARGED`, `ROW_VERIFIED`, `BOUNDED_CERTIFICATE_PASSED`, `MATHEMATICALLY_SOLVED`. Scoped numeric or conditional evidence cannot imply solved. |
| `assurance_status` | `ASSURANCE_NOT_RUN`, `ASSURANCE_FAILED`, `ASSURANCE_PASSED`. Passing requires all in-scope mandatory gates on one frozen candidate with no open P0/P1. |
| `publication_status` | `not_planned`, `planned`, `approved`, `prepared`, `committed`, `committed_recovery_required`, `rolled_back`, `failed`. A committed state never regresses. |
| `audit_status` | `AUDIT_NOT_RUN`, `AUDIT_PROCESS_FAILED`, `AUDIT_PROCESS_SUCCEEDED` plus separate `audit_verdict=ACCEPT|REJECT|BLOCKING_FINDINGS`. Reviewer process exit never determines semantic acceptance. |
| `incident_status` | Versioned incident identity; severity or `unassigned`; `OPEN`, `MITIGATED`, or `CLOSED`; affected commit/artifact refs; and closure evidence. Audit outcome does not substitute for incident closure. |

`CampaignOutcomeV3.derive` may return `MATHEMATICALLY_SOLVED` only when all required targets are checked; every mandatory obligation is verified with none rejected or unresolved; Lean declaration types and allowed axioms match; semantic review binds the same frozen target/source/declaration/toolchain; provenance and required approvals validate at the declared trust level; every authenticity requirement in the support statement is satisfied; and the evidence commit is complete. If cross-principal or cross-owner authenticity is required but unsupported, outcome derivation returns a blocked/undischarged result rather than `MATHEMATICALLY_SOLVED` or `ASSURANCE_PASSED`.

## 13. Migration and coexistence

### Physical, logical, build, and authority isolation

- Retained history is mounted or permissioned read-only under `runs-v1/`; native state lives under `runs-v2/`; native immutable content lives under `artifacts-v2/`. They use different artifact namespaces, schema registries, lock roots, runtime registries, build roots, caches, and toolchain environments.
- V1 parity execution occurs only in a hermetic disposable tree such as `tmp/v1-parity/<case>/`, against copied corpus bytes. It may create ephemeral comparison output there, but cannot update `runs-v1/`, publish durable v1 records, or admit its output as new certified history.
- Native execution and builds use attempt-scoped disposable roots such as `build-v2/<run>/<attempt>/`; staged contracts are stored outside candidate-writable trees and committed by digest. Trusted Lean acceptance uses a controller-created authenticated report channel separate from candidate-controlled stdout/stderr; any truncation fails before parsing. Compilation and report generation use the same validated absolute pinned Lean executable/toolchain. A project-controlled second `lake env` is forbidden unless its complete resolution and inputs are independently attested. V1 and v2 wheels/environments are pinned separately.
- A write fence rejects every open-for-write, rename, deletion, chmod, repair, publication, or evidence refresh targeting retained v1 history. Read locks, if needed, live outside `runs-v1/` and do not alter the corpus.
- Format selection is explicit by schema/type negotiation. No service dispatches by filename, basename, path layout, or field presence.

### One-way migration contract

1. Acquire a stable read-only snapshot of the v1 bundle and read each bounded object once.
2. Record raw byte digests and a raw root digest before interpretation.
3. Parse only through `v2.compat_v1.v1_import`, preserving exact accepted/rejected behavior.
4. Reject mixed-version or ambiguous objects; never guess repairs for malformed journals, registries, receipts, or artifacts.
5. Create a fresh v2 run ID and lineage event; emit only distinct v2 schemas and immutable refs.
6. Publish a migration report enumerating recovered defaults, normalizations, aliases, unversioned inputs, omissions, unsupported security/platform assumptions, integrity defects, intentional semantic tightening, and every blocker to certified migration.
7. Verify the original v1 tree is byte-identical. On failure, delete or quarantine only the incomplete v2 destination.

There is no v2-to-v1 downgrade and no rollback by serializing v2 state as v1. Preexisting v1 publications remain historical external facts: v2 may import their receipt and observed destination snapshot but must not claim authenticity or replayability unsupported by retained evidence.

### Migration sequence

1. **Freeze and fence:** pin v1 commit, APIs/CLI, toolchains, schemas, good/bad/incident bundles, tests, exit codes, and platform assumptions; make retained bundles read-only; disable only known destructive legacy actions.
2. **Contracts and parity:** implement `v2.contracts` and `v2.compat_v1`; capture golden accepted/rejected vectors for every retained reader; define distinct v2 types and raw-byte import reports.
3. **Authoritative storage:** implement safe filesystem primitives, immutable artifacts/snapshots, typed events, commit heads, pure projection, and evidence commits; prove import leaves source bytes unchanged.
4. **Deterministic control:** implement the reducer, serial scheduler, budgets, retry/invalidation closure, approvals, orthogonal statuses, and finalization; qualify with fake executors and model-based crash tests.
5. **Unified execution:** route command, agent, Lean, and Herdr through one supervisor/resource ledger with capability preflight, durable intents, identities, bounded streams, deadlines, reaping, attestations, and reconciliation.
6. **First native vertical slice:** execute immutable input import → command stage → Lean gate → continuation decision → state/evidence commits → injected-crash resume → inspection → non-destructive export, using only v2 records and no legacy mutation path.
7. **Trust closure:** add one-read handoffs, claims/verdicts/ledger, semantic review, obligations, approvals/provenance, compute receipts, and derived outcome; replay historical false-closure fixtures and require them to remain unsolved or blocked.
8. **Publication:** enable `v2.publication` only after approval-semantics goldens and the complete prepare/swap/fsync/commit/rollback crash matrix pass.
9. **Compatibility surfaces:** route native v2 inputs through `v2.facade`; route historical v1 inputs only to inspect/import/parity; remove every production v1 internal import outside `v2.compat_v1`.
10. **Outer adapters and retirement:** migrate regime, autonomy, and benchmarks individually as typed reducers only after their own intent, reason-code, budget, and authority gates pass; then remove v1 from production dispatch while retaining its executable-spec corpus indefinitely.

### Clean cutover criteria

Production routing moves from v1 to v2 only when all of the following are true on one frozen v2 candidate:

- consumer/API/CLI inventory is complete or every unresolved consumer is explicitly blocked from cutover;
- repository checks prove only `v2.compat_v1` imports v1 parser implementations and no native writer emits a v1 identity;
- all migration golden bundles preserve source bytes and produce classified reports;
- the native vertical slice passes schema, durability, execution, evidence, replay, and crash-recovery gates;
- known v1 false-closure, journal/projection, stale-output, process, secret, replay, and publication incidents fail closed in v2;
- `implementation_status=CODE_COMPLETE`, `full_gate_status=FULL_GATES_PASSED`, and `assurance_status=ASSURANCE_PASSED` are simultaneously evidenced for the declared support statement;
- v1 write-fence tests pass and production credentials cannot mutate retained v1 storage.

If a v2 release is withdrawn, stop new dispatch, preserve its event/artifact store, and append a typed withdrawal/recovery/compensation event that creates a new monotonic commit head whose effective state references the last validated state. A backward-readable v2 binary may read the existing head, but authority must never be rewound or repointed to an older head. Never resume v1 writing.

## 14. Acceptance gates

A skipped P0 gate fails release unless its capability is explicitly outside the declared support statement. Every lane emits machine-readable results, capability metadata, exact commit/toolchain/environment identity, complete stdout/stderr with truncation flags, and retained artifact links.

| Gate | Mandatory acceptance condition |
|---|---|
| **P0-DECISION/SCHEMAS** | Only `v2.compat_v1` imports v1 parsers; v1 paths reject writes; migrations are byte-preserving; every v2 record has unique type/version, canonical encoding, strictness policy, identity bindings, producer/minimum reader, migration path, and stable machine errors. |
| **P0-AUTHORITY/DURABILITY** | All native v2 authority-bearing status, resume, inspection, evidence, and CLI reads resolve one validated commit head. Compatibility import/parity reads instead use the bounded immutable v1 snapshot and raw-root binding required by the migration contract. Deleted projections rebuild only from valid events. Forged projections and reordered, deleted, duplicated, rolled-back, or malformed complete events are rejected. Deterministic crash injection at every intent/write/file-fsync/directory-fsync/rename/event/projection/evidence/resource boundary converges to a valid prior or next state without duplicate external effects. Only the declared unterminated-tail compatibility case is repairable. |
| **P0-FILESYSTEM/EVIDENCE** | Descriptor-relative adversarial tests reject traversal, absolute paths, source/destination symlinks, forbidden hardlinks, special files, case collisions where applicable, parent swaps, read-growth races, and concurrent writers. A verified snapshot binds campaign/run, state sequence/digest, event head, configuration/input/resource/artifact roots, and the exact retained set. |
| **P0-PROCESS/EXECUTION** | Normal exit, launch failure, timeout, quota breach, interruption, sandbox failure, cleanup failure, leader-first exit, resistant descendants, PID/start mismatch, stale registration, pidfd/capability failure, EPERM, and resource exhaustion produce typed outcomes. Every member is reauthenticated against PID, start time, process-group ID, session ID, boot/generation identity, and available pidfd/capability evidence before signaling; unauthenticated group broadcast is forbidden. Deterministic leader-death, PID/PGID/SID reuse, and orphan-ambiguity cases pass without signaling unrelated processes. No pump survives return, and terminal artifacts appear only after reconciliation. `stop_run`/`stop_all` are idempotent, resume from durable phase receipts, and are bounded by one global deadline covering freeze, process/unit termination, Herdr close, reconciliation, state commit, and evidence commit; repeated invocation converges without duplicate effects. |
| **P0-SECRETS/SANDBOX** | Untrusted execution receives the declared minimal environment, no undeclared secret, read-only authorized inputs, isolated writable output, and no network/user bus unless authorized. Attestations prove applied policy. Redaction/quarantine covers multiple and overlapping byte sequences, decoder boundaries, final flush, every retained sink, and bounded buffering. |
| **P0-CONTROL** | Scheduler queries are pure and record ready set/selection. No task starts without an adopted hash-bound plan and budget authorization. Retries have durable typed intent; invalidation is dependency-derived; gates fail closed; controller terminality, campaign completion, mathematical solution, assurance, and publication remain independently observable. |
| **P0-CAMPAIGN-CONTRACTS** | A frozen manifest/schema resolves the required-approval versus uninterrupted-one-command contradiction, requires the missing bridge DAG gate, narrows the boundary profile, assigns the correct source-challenge profile, and routes repair through proof-producing work. Parser, feature-registration, DAG-legality, approval/resume, routing, profile-scope, and proof-producing-repair tests pass. Non-proof repair output receives no mathematical closure credit. |
| **P0-HANDOFFS/CLAIMS** | Parse and hash use identical immutable bytes; all schemas resolve at manifest compilation; campaign/run/producer/consumer/source/proposal/target bindings validate. Portable claim-ID goldens pass; verdict coverage is exact; disagreements remain retained; closure rejects blocked, stale, cross-run, missing-gate, and failed-producer evidence. |
| **P0-LEAN/SEMANTICS** | One pinned hermetic Lean oracle builds the actual immutable staged project using one validated absolute toolchain identity for both compilation and report generation. A controller-created authenticated report channel is separate from candidate-controlled stdout/stderr; any truncation fails before parsing; no project-controlled second `lake env` is accepted unless complete resolution and inputs are independently attested. The exact declaration signature appears once; complete structured axiom output is target-bound; source, rendered contract, executable, dependency, invocation, and output hashes are retained. Semantic review is independent, nonmutating, round-trippable, and bound to the same target/source/declaration/toolchain. Model prose overrides neither lane. |
| **P0-OUTCOME** | `CampaignOutcomeV3.derive` returns solved only under the complete truth predicate. Every known v1 false-closure fixture remains `MATHEMATICAL_UNSOLVED`, `CONTRACT_UNDISCHARGED`, or blocked. |
| **P0-REPLAY** | Replay runs in a disposable read-only snapshot with pinned executable/toolchain/environment/sandbox inputs; mutation outside declared outputs fails; the canonical completed run is byte- and commit-identical afterward; replay evidence forms a new generation or external receipt without rewriting the certified root. |
| **P0-PUBLICATION** | The plan digest binds source bytes, canonical destination semantics, preserve semantics including an explicit order policy, and observed destination identity. `PublicationApprovalV2` binds that exact plan digest, approver/policy identity, and required authorization context. Private staging is anchored to the destination dirfd, uses the same parent and filesystem, and fails before mutation when those semantics are unavailable. Canonical destination locking and immediate under-lock revalidation occur; staged bytes are rehashed; every crash cut recovers; committed state never regresses; recovery never changes unrelated run evidence. |
| **P0-OPERATIONS** | Every invocation has prelaunch and terminal receipts containing exact argv/capabilities, provider/model where applicable, monotonic timing, process/resource identity, stream hashes/truncation, outputs, validation, compute known/unknown fields, and retry disposition. No retained invocation is ambiguously running after terminalization. |
| **P0-REVIEW/TRACEABILITY** | Every missing or opaque historical P0/P1 review payload is rerun on the same frozen candidate; complete findings, severities, attack traces, evidence refs, and closure dispositions are retained. Every test records its exact symbol, incident/invariant ID, real/fake boundary, platform capabilities, schema versions, result artifacts, and `proves`/`does_not_prove`. No-open-P0/P1 claims are derived only from this complete trace. |
| **P0-MIGRATION** | Every inventoried v1 golden vector has the same compatibility classification, DTO values, deterministic ordering, documented error class, status, and exit mapping. Deliberate v1 defects are reported, not adopted. Historical corruption is quarantined, never silently repaired. |
| **P1-ORCHESTRATION** | Regime/autonomy/benchmark adapters, if included in the support statement, use typed reducers and committed campaign/evidence IDs; durable intents, reason codes, budgets, operator/AFK authority, cancellation, and crash/concurrency transitions have acceptance coverage. |
| **P1-TOOLING/END-TO-END** | Schema/property, model-based crash, subprocess, adversarial-filesystem, privileged-Linux, real-toolchain, end-to-end security, mutation/fuzz, migration-golden, and publication-fault lanes all pass for the declared capabilities. |
| **RELEASE EVIDENCE** | The release report distinguishes passed, failed, unsupported, quarantined, and capability-skipped contracts and records `proves`/`does_not_prove`. Release requires `implementation_status=CODE_COMPLETE`, `full_gate_status=FULL_GATES_PASSED`, and `assurance_status=ASSURANCE_PASSED` on one immutable commit. A theorem-claiming product additionally requires the relevant `CAMPAIGN_COMPLETE` and `MATHEMATICALLY_SOLVED` receipts; these labels never substitute for one another. |

Current scope truth remains: **global `implementation_status=CODE_COMPLETE` is not established; `full_gate_status=FULL_GATES_PASSED` is not established; CMV is `MATHEMATICAL_UNSOLVED`; and global assurance is `ASSURANCE_NOT_RUN` until the frozen-candidate gates execute.**

## 15. Prioritized next mathematical work

Work is receipt-first and dependency-ordered. A downstream item may begin experimentally, but it cannot contribute closure until every predecessor is accepted and hash-bound in the claim DAG.

### P0 — Establish formal and source authority

1. **Freeze evidence and toolchains.** Pin the repository commit, Lean/Mathlib manifest, Python, mpmath, MPFR/ABI, CMV/Morgan sources, generated certificates, reports, and manifests; hash every retained input.
2. **Verify the current Lean baseline.** Add one root importing every public target; run a clean target-including build; retain exact theorem types, import DAG, source/toolchain hashes, complete output, `#check`, `#print axioms`, and scans for `sorry`, `admit`, custom axioms, unsafe/native substitution, and unreachable declarations. Until this passes, all Lean claims remain `pending execution`.
3. **Normalize claim authority.** Build a typed claim DAG with distinct modeled, conditional, source-derived, numeric, interval, and Lean-receipted classes; promotion records override rhetoric, audit overlays override producers, and permanent corrections are append-only.
4. **Lock the source contract.** Define the source admissible/finite-perimeter class, strip density including the interface convention, weighted area, weighted reduced-boundary perimeter, regular type-(iii)/(iv) predicates, branches, endpoints, and exact cited premises. Human/source authority must approve this contract before source-level formalization.
5. **Canonicalize the scalar specification.** Define one namespaced AST/API for segment area, length, `θOf`, `arc`, `g`, E3/E4 formulas, branch tags, dimensions, and domains; generate Lean declarations, Python fixtures, and reports from it; isolate junk totalizations.

### P1 — Close the analytic and certificate layer

6. **Package the inverse-area map.** Replace witness-centric `θOf` with an order/homeomorphism equivalence `(0,π) ≃o (0,∞)` or equivalent; derive inverse, continuity, differentiability, asymptotic APIs, and positive-chord scaling with `x=A/L²` for `L>0`.
7. **Complete the sharp analytic API.** Expose `deriv g`; define `θstar`, `xstar`, and `k:=3 cos θstar`; prove `k>0`, `IsLeast (g '' Ioi 0)`, unique argmin, exact attainment, and the strict/equality/failure criterion: strict universal comparison only for `λ>1/k`, equality at `(1/k,xstar)`, and failure below.
8. **Rebuild proof-producing certificates.** Replace handwritten decimal ladders with generated exact certificates replayed by a small Lean kernel-facing interval library. Preserve compatibility theorem names and exact rational cutoffs, while distinguishing the sharp threshold from the sufficient cutoff `1.2581840884`.

### P1 — Close model geometry before source transfer

9. **Retain and audit explicit geometry.** Preserve cap/core carriers, Euclidean `WithLp 2`, volume formulas, frontier traces, attachment/exposure, and the major replacement branch; remove duplicated curve, seam, and finite-union lemmas.
10. **Verify the topological-frontier bridge.** Receipt-audit `FrontierPerimeter`: primitive pushforwards equal Euclidean H¹ restrictions, overlaps are H¹-null, and constructor boundary sums equal `FrontierMeasure`. Keep this theorem explicitly topological-frontier scoped.
11. **Build the BV/reduced-boundary bridge.** Prove finite perimeter for all regular assemblies; identify reduced boundary and topological frontier H¹-a.e.; establish density traces at the interface; prove equality with the source weighted perimeter. No source theorem may bypass this dependency.
12. **Define source-faithful type IV.** Separate `0<h<1` source-regular parameters from modeled closure `h=1`; prove normalization up to density-preserving isometry, parameter existence/uniqueness, Snell/conormal incidence, and preservation of carrier, area, perimeter, and admissibility.
13. **Define source-faithful type III.** Implement all E3 major/semicircular/minor branches, including the corrected `h=1/2` degeneration; prove carrier validity, finite perimeter, exact formulas, branch transitions, admissibility, and source correspondence.

### P2 — Close global comparison and minimality

14. **Formalize equal-area topology.** Prove folds, complete root sets, descending-root existence, second-root criterion, least-perimeter selection, endpoint equality cases, and exact weighted-area matching. Numerical continuation or a single root is not completeness evidence.
15. **Formalize the full-domain scalar sign.** Port the low-density scaled remainder, compact directed-MPFR certificate, and large-density tail into one kernel-checked `Γ<0` theorem for all `λ>1`, with explicit joins at `17/16` and `3/2` and uniform boundary handling. The quarantined `verify_full_domain.py` contributes no evidence.
16. **Construct the source competitor.** Turn the selected E3 root into an actual source-admissible, exactly equal-weighted-area competitor and prove its source reduced-boundary perimeter is strictly lower than the E4 candidate.
17. **Transfer minimality quantifiers.** Prove that a source minimizer among **all** equal-area finite-perimeter competitors induces the modeled contradiction interface; do not encode this as an assumption-shaped compatibility field or restrict the quantifier to circular constructors.
18. **Export terminal theorems separately.** Publish distinct declarations for: analytic sharp criterion; modeled rational-cutoff exclusion; conditional source cutoff exclusion; all-`λ` modeled exclusion; all-`λ` source type-(iv) exclusion; type-(iii) result; and final CMV classification. Each declaration exposes exact domains, perimeter semantics, candidate universe, endpoint strictness, and dependency receipts.
19. **Generate closure documentation.** Derive reports, theorem snippets, cutoffs, axiom manifests, and unresolved matrices from the accepted claim DAG; archive stale notes and unsound verifiers; point configured success only to a compiled audited theorem of exactly matching scope.

The full CMV theorem remains blocked until steps 1–17 are dependency-closed. Existing scalar, interval, coordinate-model, conditional, and source-derived artifacts retain their audited evidence classes and cannot be upgraded by aggregation.

## 16. Non-goals and deferred capabilities

### Explicit non-goals for the initial native core

- No in-place refactor of `runtime.py`, `features.py`, `agent_runner.py`, `command.py`, registry families, `regime.py`, or `autonomy.py`. Native behavior is reimplemented behind the interfaces in §12.
- No new v1 artifact, field, status, feature ID, receipt, repair behavior, writer, or broadened reader. No migration edits its source bundle; no v2 writer uses a v1 schema identity.
- No v2-to-v1 downgrade, fallback writer, or production continuation that emits uncertified v1 history.
- No parallel DAG execution. Legacy `max_parallel` is parsed only for compatibility/migration reporting; it is not represented as active behavior.
- No distributed controller, cross-host lease, network-filesystem guarantee, or portability beyond an explicitly tested local POSIX/Linux capability contract.
- No live-symlink certified inputs, mutable replay, arbitrary absolute output/receipt fallback, lexical-only publication authorization, or raw path strings as evidence/capabilities.
- No custom third-party feature executors in certified resume/replay until implementation digests, schemas, capability manifests, and migration contracts are enforced.
- No model authority over tools, provider/model selection, timeout, attempts, parallelism, failure policy, security grants, continuation, closure, publication, or solved status.
- No ambient substring scan as authorization and no broad destructive workspace secret deletion as the primary control. Optional lint must be explicitly non-authoritative.
- No automatic repair of malformed historical journals, registries, receipts, or artifacts. Quarantine and report; preserve only the exact v1 unterminated-tail compatibility rule for offline inspection.
- No assurance inferred from exit code, filename, basename, configured verifier label, stage count, model prose, or shallow schema acceptance. Failed producers receive no closure credit.
- No claim that v2 proves mathematical truth or informal/formal equivalence. Lean/kernel evidence establishes only the exact declaration relative to its axioms; independent expert/human semantic authority remains required where specified.
- No full-source CMV theorem claim. Current status remains `MATHEMATICAL_UNSOLVED` until the source-faithful dependency chain and formal receipts close.

### Deferred until the minimal vertical slice and P0 gates pass

- Parallel isolated branches, deterministic merge/conflict rules, resource-cap scheduling, and parallel crash semantics.
- Distributed execution, remote leases, signed remote attestation, shared/NFS storage semantics, and cross-principal authenticity mechanisms. Signatures/MACs require an approved ownership/export threat model first.
- Native regime, autonomy, benchmark, successor-plan execution, and project-wide strategy automation; each requires its own typed reducer and acceptance coverage.
- Third-party feature plugins, custom executors, and replay across changed implementations/toolchains.
- Non-Linux containment equivalents and broad filesystem portability.
- Broad arbitrary-filesystem publication and cross-owner exports.
- Canonical semantic JSON hashing unless cross-encoding identity or signing requires it; raw-byte identity remains sufficient when parse and hash use the same immutable bytes.
- Broad regression/TensorFlow expansion, planner-autonomous regression use, dashboards, terminal cosmetics, and speculative feature work.
- Expensive Terra/Sol mathematical exploration before deterministic source, graph, budget, build, and Lean feasibility gates pass.
- Automatic execution of successor plans or automatic adjudication of ambiguous v1 migrations.
- Final source-level type-(iii), type-(iv), and CMV closure until the dependency-ordered work in §15 is complete; modeled, conditional, numeric, and interval results remain separately labeled.

Keep assessment, bounded loaders, deterministic linear baseline, explicit non-proof labeling, and content-bound receipts. Move all parsing/fitting to a resource-limited subprocess, stream or preflight formats before allocation, add group/time-aware split contracts and a separate untouched test set, provide a unified inference bundle, lock/fsync output publication, and version the receipt. DEFER tensorflow_dense by default until the evaluation and resource-isolation requirements are met; make linear the default. | deterministic |
| `src/campaign_builder/runtime.py` | 2025 | source/engine | Owns the v1 campaign controller lifecycle: create/retain a run, serialize stage-DAG execution, checkpoint/restore the mutable workspace, persist journal/state/evidence, route retries/revisions, manage Herdr resources, approve typed research-to-build handoffs, import provenance-pinned artifacts, verify bundles, and replay retained command verifiers. The module is also the de facto filesystem schema and recovery protocol for a run. | REWRITE/DECOMPOSE. Preserve the public concepts and proven security checks, but replace this monolithic controller with separately versioned RunStore, StateMachine, Scheduler, WorkspaceTransaction, EvidenceIndex, ResourceManager, HandoffApproval, ProvenanceImporter, and ReplayService. Provide a v1 read-only/migration adapter; do not carry forward ad hoc full-state journaling or live-workspace replay semantics. | deterministic — all orchestration, validation, hashing, scheduling, recovery, approval digest binding, and replay policy must remain non-LLM; human is required only for manual handoff approval and exceptional corruption adjudication. |
| `src/campaign_builder/sandbox.py` | 639 | source/execution | Builds Linux systemd-run plus bubblewrap sandbox invocations, constructs filtered environments and provenance metadata, controls transient units, measures workspace usage, detects/removes secret-bearing workspace entries, and fingerprints executable/dependency manifests. | REWRITE/HARDEN WITH HUMAN SECURITY REVIEW. Preserve the policy concepts, systemd cgroup plus mount-namespace defense in depth, deterministic environment, and provenance metadata. Replace destructive post-hoc secret scanning with secret non-injection/mounted secret handles and taint-aware artifact publication; make workspace quotas event/quota-backed where possible; cache immutable toolchain digests; verify systemctl states explicitly; use fd-relative no-follow traversal; version metadata and document the Linux capability contract. | human |
| `src/campaign_builder/semantic.py` | 169 | source/evidence | Parses a strict schema-v1 handoff describing an independent informal-to-Lean semantic comparison, classifies the relationship, aggregates issue categories, and serializes the review body. REWRITE as a campaign-bound, source/Lean-digest-bound schema with declaration identity, toolchain/elaboration receipt, reviewer provenance/independence metadata, machine-readable issue codes/locations, relation-specific validation, resource bounds, migrations, and a truly round-trippable serializer that includes schema_version. Semantic judgment remains outside deterministic parsing and needs expert assurance. | human for authoritative informal-to-formal equivalence judgment; deterministic for schema validation and Lean artifact binding, with Sol usable only as a nonauthoritative reviewer |
| `src/campaign_builder/terminal_status.py` | 196 | source/control | Non-durable terminal presentation utilities: elapsed formatting, ANSI/color/width/truncation helpers, a thread-driven per-role heartbeat spinner that coexists with subprocess output, and an aggregate controller DAG status renderer for TTY and plain streams. | KEEP with localized rewrite. Preserve non-durable separation and plain-output deduplication. Add an explicit context-manager lifecycle, idempotent start/finish, shared stream renderer/lock, wcwidth-style cell accounting, richer terminal states, and injected clock/width for deterministic tests. Do not let this module participate in campaign state or evidence. | deterministic |

## 20. Machine companion

`V1_SUPPORT.json` is normative for future agents. It contains full structured per-file audits, grouped line findings, all ten campaign summaries, detailed campaign slice audits, retained review-history audits, model-routing policy, playbook contracts, rebuild decision, and complete path/line/byte/digest manifests. This Markdown is a dense projection; when the two differ in granularity, use the JSON evidence fields and then re-open the cited source at its recorded digest.
